  <header class="documentation-header">
    <div class="wrap documentation-header-inner">
      <a id="documentationBack" class="documentation-back" href="./index.html?course=inverse-square#course">← Zurück zum Lernweg</a>
      <p class="eyebrow">GeoGebra-Trainer · Klausurdokumentation</p>
      <h1>Für die Klausur dokumentieren</h1>
      <p>Schreibe knapp, aber nachvollziehbar auf, was du mit GeoGebra ausgewertet und physikalisch gedeutet hast.</p>
    </div>
  </header>

  <main id="main" class="documentation-main wrap">
    <section class="documentation-intro" aria-labelledby="documentation-intro-title">
      <p class="mini-label">Allgemeine Anleitung</p>
      <h2 id="documentation-intro-title">Was eine gute Dokumentation zeigt</h2>
      <p>Deine Dokumentation soll zeigen, welche Daten du ausgewertet hast, was du mit GeoGebra berechnet hast und wie du daraus ein physikalisches Ergebnis gewinnst. Schreibe so viel auf, dass dein Rechenweg nachvollziehbar bleibt – auch dann, wenn dir ein Fehler unterlaufen ist.</p>
      <p>Du musst weder jeden Fingertipp beschreiben noch eine vorgegebene Messwerttabelle abschreiben. Wichtig sind die verwendeten Größen und Einheiten, die entscheidenden Eingaben mit ihren Zellbereichen, die Ergebnisse und eine begründete Schlussfolgerung.</p>
      <ol id="documentationGuide" class="documentation-guide"><li>Nenne Messgrößen, Einheiten und die verwendete Datenauswahl; schreibe keine vollständige Tabelle ab.</li><li>Notiere die entscheidenden GeoGebra-Eingaben mit Zellbereich und das Ergebnis der Auswertung.</li><li>Übertrage die numerische GeoGebra-Funktion in eine physikalische Formel mit Größen und Einheiten.</li><li>Zeige eine eingesetzte Abweichungsrechnung, die größte Abweichung und die verwendete Fehlergrenze.</li><li>Formuliere eine vorsichtige Vereinbarkeitsaussage; Messdaten können ein Modell stützen, aber nicht beweisen.</li></ol>
      <aside class="documentation-note">
        <strong>Kurz und fachlich:</strong> Runde Ergebnisse sinnvoll, rechne in GeoGebra mit den ungerundeten Werten weiter und beschreibe Abweichungen mit „oberhalb“ oder „unterhalb“. Beim Fehlervergleich zählt ihre Größe unabhängig von der Richtung.
      </aside>
    </section>

    <section class="documentation-workspace" aria-labelledby="documentation-selection-title">
      <div class="section-heading documentation-heading">
        <div>
          <p class="eyebrow">Beispiel auswählen</p>
          <h2 id="documentation-selection-title">Welchen Zusammenhang dokumentierst du?</h2>
        </div>
        <p class="section-intro">Die Muster sind vollständig ausgeführt. Im Lernmodus schreibst du dieselben fünf Abschnitte selbst auf Papier und vergleichst anschließend mit der Musterlösung.</p>
      </div>

      <div id="documentationExamples" class="documentation-example-picker" role="group" aria-label="Dokumentationsbeispiel auswählen"><button type="button" class="documentation-example-button" data-example-id="inverse-square" aria-pressed="false"><span>Umgekehrtes Quadratgesetz</span><small>Coulomb-Versuch · Potenzregression</small></button><button type="button" class="documentation-example-button" data-example-id="proportional-power" aria-pressed="false"><span>Direkte Proportionalität · Potenzregression</span><small>Kondensator-Versuch · Potenzregression</small></button><button type="button" class="documentation-example-button" data-example-id="proportional-constants" aria-pressed="false"><span>Direkte Proportionalität · Konstantenverfahren</span><small>Kondensator-Versuch · Konstantenverfahren</small></button><button type="button" class="documentation-example-button" data-example-id="proportional-linear" aria-pressed="false"><span>Direkte Proportionalität · Lineare Regression</span><small>Kondensator-Versuch · Lineare Regression</small></button><button type="button" class="documentation-example-button" data-example-id="capacitor-exponential" aria-pressed="true"><span>Kondensator-Aufladung · Exponentialregression</span><small>Kondensator-Aufladung · Exponentialregression</small></button></div>

        <div class="mode-control">

      <section id="documentationModel" class="documentation-view" aria-label="Ausgeführte Musterdokumentation"><article id="model-inverse-square" class="documentation-paper documentation-model-paper" data-example-id="inverse-square" hidden="">
    <header class="documentation-paper-header">
      <div>
        <p class="mini-label">Ausgeführtes Muster</p>
        <span class="documentation-paper-kicker">Coulomb-Versuch · Potenzregression</span>
        <h2>Umgekehrtes Quadratgesetz</h2>
      </div>
      <div class="documentation-paper-equation"><mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" display="true" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="F\propto\frac{1}{r^2}" display="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="F"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D439">𝐹</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo space="4" data-latex="\propto"><mjx-c class="mjx-c221D">∝</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mfrac space="4" data-latex="\frac{1}{r^2}"><mjx-frac type="d"><mjx-num><mjx-nstrut type="d"></mjx-nstrut><mjx-mn data-latex="1"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31">1</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-num><mjx-dbox><mjx-dtable><mjx-line type="d"></mjx-line><mjx-row><mjx-den><mjx-dstrut type="d"></mjx-dstrut><mjx-msup data-latex="r^2"><mjx-mi data-latex="r"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D45F">𝑟</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: 0.289em;"><mjx-mn size="s" data-latex="2"><mjx-c class="mjx-c32">2</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-script></mjx-msup></mjx-den></mjx-row></mjx-dtable></mjx-dbox></mjx-frac></mjx-mfrac></mjx-math></mjx-container></div>
    </header>
    <p class="documentation-example-introduction">Dieses Beispiel dokumentiert sechs Messpaare aus dem Coulomb-Versuch.</p>
    <div class="documentation-model-sections">
      
        <section class="documentation-section" data-section-id="data">
          <h3>1. Daten und Einheiten</h3>
          <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <p>Ich werte sechs Messpaare aus dem Coulomb-Versuch aus.</p>
        <ul>
          <li><strong>A1:A6:</strong> Abstände <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="r" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="r"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D45F">𝑟</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> aus der Messwerttabelle eingetragen; Einheit <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="\mathrm{cm}" class="NCM-N"><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm{cm}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="cm"><mjx-c class="mjx-c63" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.448em;">c</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c6D" style="padding-top: 0.448em;">m</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container>.</li>
          <li><strong>B1:B6:</strong> zugehörige Kräfte <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="F" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="F"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D439">𝐹</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> eingetragen; Einheit <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="\mathrm{mN}" class="NCM-N"><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm{mN}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="mN"><mjx-c class="mjx-c6D" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.683em;">m</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c4E" style="padding-top: 0.683em;">N</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container>.</li>
          <li><strong>C1:</strong> <code>=(A1,B1)</code>, bis C6 ausgefüllt → Punkte <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="(r,F)" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mo data-latex="("><mjx-c class="mjx-c28">(</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi data-latex="r"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D45F">𝑟</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi space="2" data-latex="F"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D439">𝐹</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex=")"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29">)</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-math></mjx-container>.</li>
        </ul></div>
        </section>
      
        <section class="documentation-section" data-section-id="geogebra">
          <h3>2. GeoGebra-Auswertung</h3>
          <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <p>Für die Punkte C1:C6 führe ich eine Potenzregression durch:</p>
        <pre><code>F(x)=TrendPot(C1:C6)</code></pre>
        <p>GeoGebra liefert näherungsweise:</p>
        <p class="display-equation"><mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" display="true" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="F(x)=28{,}9022\,x^{-2{,}0750}." class="mjx-c1D465">𝑥</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex=")"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29">)</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mo space="4" data-latex="="><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D">=</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="4" data-latex="28{,}902"><mjx-c class="mjx-c32" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">2</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c38" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">8</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">,</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c39" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">9</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-msup data-latex="x^{-2{,}0750}"><mjx-mi data-latex="x"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D465">𝑥</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: 0.413em;"><mjx-texatom size="s" data-latex="{-2{,}0750}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex="-"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2212">−</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn data-latex="2{,}075"><mjx-c class="mjx-c32" noic="true" style="padding-top: class="mjx-c30">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-texatom></mjx-script></mjx-msup><mjx-mo data-latex="."><mjx-c class="mjx-c2E">.</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-math></mjx-container></p>
        <p>Die Funktion <code>F</code> berechnet den Zahlenwert der Kraft; <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="x" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="x"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D465">𝑥</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> steht für den Zahlenwert des Abstands in Zentimetern.</p></div>
        </section>
      
        <section class="documentation-section" data-section-id="physical">
          <h3>3. Physikalische Formel und Parameter</h3>
          <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <p>Damit Einheiten eindeutig bleiben, schreibe ich die Regression mit einem normierten Abstand:</p>
        <p class="display-equation"><mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" display="true" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math         <p>Der Klammerausdruck ist einheitenfrei. Der Faktor trägt deshalb die Einheit <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="\mathrm{mN}" class="NCM-N"><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm{mN}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="mN"><mjx-c class="mjx-c6D" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.683em;">m</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c4E" style="padding-top: 0.683em;">N</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container>.</p></div>
        </section>
      
        <section class="documentation-section" data-section-id="deviations">
          <h3>4. Abweichungen und Vergleichsregel</h3>
          <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <ul>
          <li><strong>D1:</strong> <code>=F(A1)</code>, bis D6 ausgefüllt → Modellwerte.</li>
          <li><strong>E1:</strong> <code>=(B1-D1)/D1*100</code>, bis E6 ausgefüllt → relative Modellabweichungen.</li>
        </ul>
        <p>Die größte Modellabweichung liegt bei <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="r=12{,}4\,\mathrm{cm}" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="r"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D45F">𝑟</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-break size="4"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="="><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D">=</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="4" data-latex="12"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c32" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">2</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-texatom data-latex="{,}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-texatom><mjx-mn data-latex="4"><mjx-c class="mjx-c34">4</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm{cm}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="cm"><mjx-c class="mjx-c63" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.448em;">c</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c6D" style="padding-top: 0.448em;">m</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container>:</p>
        <p class="display-equation"><mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" display="true" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math         <p>Der Messwert liegt dort um etwa <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="15{,}7\,\%" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="15"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c35" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">5</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-texatom data-latex="{,}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-texatom><mjx-mn data-latex="7"><mjx-c class="mjx-c37">7</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> über dem Modellwert. Als größter relativer Einzelfehler wird <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="0{,}01/0{,}06\cdot100\,\%\approx16{,}7\,\%" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="0"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-texatom data-latex="{,}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-texatom><mjx-mn data-latex="01"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mo data-latex="/"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2F">/</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn data-latex="0"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-texatom data-latex="{,}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-texatom><mjx-mn data-latex="06"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c36" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">6</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="3"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="\cdot"><mjx-c class="mjx-c22C5">⋅</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="3" data-latex="100"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-break size="4"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="\approx"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2248">≈</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="4" data-latex="16"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c36" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">6</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-texatom data-latex="{,}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-texatom><mjx-mn data-latex="7"><mjx-c class="mjx-c37">7</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> verwendet.</p></div>
        </section>
      
        <section class="documentation-section" data-section-id="conclusion">
          <h3>5. Begründete Schlussfolgerung</h3>
          <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <p>Der Exponent <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="-2{,}0750" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mo data-latex="-"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2212">−</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn data-latex="2{,}075"><mjx-c class="mjx-c32" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.676em;">2</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.676em;">,</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.676em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c37" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.676em;">7</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c35" style="padding-top: 0.676em;">5</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mn data-latex="0"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-math></mjx-container> liegt nahe bei <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="-2" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mo data-latex="-"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2212">−</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn data-latex="2"><mjx-c class="mjx-c32">2</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-math></mjx-container>, und die größte Modellabweichung bleibt unter der verwendeten Fehlergrenze von <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="16{,}7\,\%" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="16"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c36" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">6</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-texatom data-latex="{,}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-texatom><mjx-mn data-latex="7"><mjx-c class="mjx-c37">7</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container>. Die Messwerte sind in dieser vereinfachten Betrachtung mit einem umgekehrten Quadratgesetz vereinbar. Sie beweisen den Zusammenhang nicht.</p></div>
        </section>
      
    </div>
    <p class="documentation-paper-footnote">Muster für eine knappe Klausurdokumentation. GeoGebra-Befehle und Zellbereiche machen den Rechenweg nachvollziehbar.</p>
  </article><article id="model-proportional-power" class="documentation-paper documentation-model-paper" data-example-id="proportional-power" hidden="">
    <header class="documentation-paper-header">
      <div>
        <p class="mini-label">Ausgeführtes Muster</p>
        <span class="documentation-paper-kicker">Kondensator-Versuch · Potenzregression</span>
        <h2>Direkte Proportionalität · Potenzregression</h2>
      </div>
      <div class="documentation-paper-equation"><mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" display="true" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="Q=C\cdot U" display="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="Q"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D444">𝑄</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo space="4" data-latex="="><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D">=</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi space="4" data-latex="C"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D436">𝐶</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo space="3" data-latex="\cdot"><mjx-c class="mjx-c22C5">⋅</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi space="3" data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container></div>
    </header>
    <p class="documentation-example-introduction">Dieses Beispiel dokumentiert fünf Messpaare eines Kondensatorversuchs.</p>
    <div class="documentation-model-sections">
      
        <section class="documentation-section" data-section-id="data">
          <h3>1. Daten und Einheiten</h3>
          <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <p>Ich werte fünf Messpaare eines Kondensatorversuchs aus.</p>
        <ul>
          <li><strong>A1:A5:</strong> Spannungen <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="U" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> eingetragen; Einheit <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="\mathrm{V}" class="NCM-N"><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm{V}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="V"><mjx-c class="mjx-c56">V</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container>.</li>
          <li><strong>B1:B5:</strong> zugehörige Ladungszahlen <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="Q/(10^{-8}\,\mathrm C)" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="Q"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D444">𝑄</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex="/"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2F">/</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mo data-latex="("><mjx-c class="mjx-c28">(</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-msup data-latex="10^{-8}"><mjx-mn data-latex="10"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-script style="vertical-align: 0.393em;"><mjx-texatom size="s" data-latex="{-8}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex="-"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2212">−</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn data-latex="8"><mjx-c class="mjx-c38">8</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-texatom></mjx-script></mjx-msup><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm C" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="C"><mjx-c class="mjx-c43">C</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom><mjx-mo data-latex=")"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29">)</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-math></mjx-container> eingetragen.</li>
          <li><strong>C1:</strong> <code>=(A1,B1)</code>, bis C5 ausgefüllt → Punkte <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="(U,Q)" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mo data-latex="("><mjx-c class="mjx-c28">(</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi space="2" data-latex="Q"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D444">𝑄</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex=")"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29">)</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-math></mjx-container>.</li>
        </ul></div>
        </section>
      
        <section class="documentation-section" data-section-id="geogebra">
          <h3>2. GeoGebra-Auswertung</h3>
          <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <p>Für die Punkte C1:C5 führe ich eine Potenzregression durch:</p>
        <pre><code>Q(x)=TrendPot(C1:C5)</code></pre>
        <p>GeoGebra liefert:</p>
        <p class="display-equation"><mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" display="true" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="Q(x)\approx0{,}0393011\,x^{1{,}011566}." display="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="Q"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D444">𝑄</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex="("><mjx-c class="mjx-c28">(</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi data-latex="x"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D465">𝑥</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex=")"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29">)</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mo space="4" data-latex="\approx"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2248">≈</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="4" data-latex="0{,}039"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">,</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c33" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">3</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c39" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">9</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mn data-latex="3011"><mjx-c class="mjx-c33" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">3</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-msup data-latex="x^{1{,}011566}"><mjx-mi data-latex="x"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D465">𝑥</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: 0.413em;"><mjx-texatom size="s" data-latex="{1{,}011566}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mn data-latex="1{,}011"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">,</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mn data-latex="566"><mjx-c class="mjx-c35" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">5</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c36" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">6</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c36" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">6</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-texatom></mjx-script></mjx-msup><mjx-mo data-latex="."><mjx-c class="mjx-c2E">.</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-math></mjx-container></p>
        <p><code>Q</code> ist der Funktionsname für die Ladung; <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="x" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="x"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D465">𝑥</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> steht für den Zahlenwert der Spannung in Volt.</p></div>
        </section>
      
        <section class="documentation-section" data-section-id="physical">
          <h3>3. Physikalische Formel und Parameter</h3>
          <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <p>Mit Größen und Einheiten lautet die Regressionsfunktion:</p>
        <p class="display-equation"><mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" display="true" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="Q(U)\approx0{,}0393011\cdot10^{-8}\,\mathrm C\left(\frac{U}{1\,\mathrm V}\right)^{1{,}011566}." display="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="Q"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D444">𝑄</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex="("><mjx-c class="mjx-c28">(</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex=")"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29">)</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mo space="4" data-latex="\approx"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2248">≈</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="4" data-latex="0{,}039"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" class="mjx-c33" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">3</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c39" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">9</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mn data-latex="3011"><mjx-c class="mjx-c33" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">3</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mo space="3" data-latex="\cdot"><mjx-c class="mjx-c22C5">⋅</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-msup space="3" data-latex="10^{-8}"><mjx-mn data-latex="10"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-script style="vertical-align: 0.413em;"><mjx-texatom size="s" data-latex="{-8}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex="-"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2212">−</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn data-latex="8"><mjx-c class="mjx-c38">8</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-texatom></mjx-script></mjx-msup><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm C" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="C"><mjx-c class="mjx-c43">C</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom><mjx-msup data-latex="\left(\frac{U}{1\,\mathrm V}\right)^{1{,}011566}"><mjx-mrow data-latex="\left(\frac{U}{1\,\mathrm V}\right)"><mjx-mo data-latex="\left("><mjx-c class="mjx-c28 NCM-S5">(</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mfrac data-latex="\frac{U}{1\,\mathrm V}"><mjx-frac type="d"><mjx-num><mjx-nstrut NCM-S5">)</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-mrow><mjx-script style="vertical-align: 1.086em;"><mjx-texatom size="s" data-latex="{1{,}011566}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mn data-latex="1{,}011"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" class="mjx-c35" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">5</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c36" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">6</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c36" style="padding-top:         <p>Der freie Faktor ist hier noch nicht automatisch eine Kapazität: Der Exponent wurde frei bestimmt und ist nicht exakt <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="1" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="1"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31">1</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-math></mjx-container>.</p></div>
        </section>
      
        <section class="documentation-section" data-section-id="deviations">
          <h3>4. Abweichungen und Vergleichsregel</h3>
          <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <ul>
          <li><strong>D1:</strong> <code>=Q(A1)</code>, bis D5 ausgefüllt → Modellwerte.</li>
          <li><strong>E1:</strong> <code>=(B1-D1)/D1*100</code>, bis E5 ausgefüllt → Modellabweichungen.</li>
        </ul>
        <p>Bei <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="U=100\,\mathrm V" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-break size="4"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="="><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D">=</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="4" data-latex="100"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm V" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="V"><mjx-c class="mjx-c56">V</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container> liegt der Messwert um etwa <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="3{,}74\,\%" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="3"><mjx-c class="mjx-c33">3</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-texatom data-latex="{,}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-texatom><mjx-mn data-latex="74"><mjx-c class="mjx-c37" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">7</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c34" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">4</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> über dem Modellwert. Der Exponent liegt um etwa <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="1{,}16\,\%" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="1"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31">1</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-texatom data-latex="{,}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-texatom><mjx-mn data-latex="16"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c36" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">6</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> über <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="1" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="1"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31">1</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-math></mjx-container>. Aus <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="5/50\cdot100\,\%=10\,\%" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="5"><mjx-c class="mjx-c35">5</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mo data-latex="/"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2F">/</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn data-latex="50"><mjx-c class="mjx-c35" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">5</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="3"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="\cdot"><mjx-c class="mjx-c22C5">⋅</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="3" data-latex="100"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-break size="4"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="="><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D">=</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="4" data-latex="10"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> und <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="0{,}1/2{,}0\cdot100\,\%=5\,\%" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="0"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-texatom data-latex="{,}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-texatom><mjx-mn data-latex="1"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31">1</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mo data-latex="/"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2F">/</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn data-latex="2"><mjx-c class="mjx-c32">2</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-texatom data-latex="{,}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-texatom><mjx-mn data-latex="0"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="3"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="\cdot"><mjx-c class="mjx-c22C5">⋅</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="3" data-latex="100"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-break size="4"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="="><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D">=</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="4" data-latex="5"><mjx-c class="mjx-c35">5</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> folgt als größter relativer Einzelfehler <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="10\,\%" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="10"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container>.</p></div>
        </section>
      
        <section class="documentation-section" data-section-id="conclusion">
          <h3>5. Begründete Schlussfolgerung</h3>
          <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <p>Die Abweichung des Exponenten von <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="1" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="1"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31">1</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-math></mjx-container> und die größte Modellabweichung liegen unter <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="10\,\%" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="10"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container>. Nach der Methode des größten Einzelfehlers können beide durch Messfehler erklärt werden. Die Daten sind mit <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="Q\propto U" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="Q"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D444">𝑄</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-break size="4"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="\propto"><mjx-c class="mjx-c221D">∝</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi space="4" data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> vereinbar, beweisen die Proportionalität aber nicht.</p></div>
        </section>
      
    </div>
    <p class="documentation-paper-footnote">Muster für eine knappe Klausurdokumentation. GeoGebra-Befehle und Zellbereiche machen den Rechenweg nachvollziehbar.</p>
  </article><article id="model-proportional-constants" class="documentation-paper documentation-model-paper" data-example-id="proportional-constants" hidden="">
    <header class="documentation-paper-header">
      <div>
        <p class="mini-label">Ausgeführtes Muster</p>
        <span class="documentation-paper-kicker">Kondensator-Versuch · Konstantenverfahren</span>
        <h2>Direkte Proportionalität · Konstantenverfahren</h2>
      </div>
      <div class="documentation-paper-equation"><mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" display="true" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="Q=C\cdot U" display="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="Q"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D444">𝑄</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo space="4" data-latex="="><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D">=</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi space="4" data-latex="C"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D436">𝐶</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo space="3" data-latex="\cdot"><mjx-c class="mjx-c22C5">⋅</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi space="3" data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container></div>
    </header>
    <p class="documentation-example-introduction">Dieses Beispiel dokumentiert die Prüfung einer konstanten Kapazität mit fünf Messpaaren.</p>
    <div class="documentation-model-sections">
      
        <section class="documentation-section" data-section-id="data">
          <h3>1. Daten und Einheiten</h3>
          <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <p>Ich werte fünf Messpaare eines Kondensatorversuchs aus.</p>
        <ul>
          <li><strong>A1:A5:</strong> Spannungen <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="U" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> eingetragen; Einheit <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="\mathrm V" class="NCM-N"><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm V" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="V"><mjx-c class="mjx-c56">V</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container>.</li>
          <li><strong>B1:B5:</strong> Ladungszahlen <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="Q/(10^{-8}\,\mathrm C)" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="Q"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D444">𝑄</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex="/"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2F">/</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mo data-latex="("><mjx-c class="mjx-c28">(</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-msup data-latex="10^{-8}"><mjx-mn data-latex="10"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-script style="vertical-align: 0.393em;"><mjx-texatom size="s" data-latex="{-8}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex="-"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2212">−</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn data-latex="8"><mjx-c class="mjx-c38">8</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-texatom></mjx-script></mjx-msup><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm C" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="C"><mjx-c class="mjx-c43">C</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom><mjx-mo data-latex=")"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29">)</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-math></mjx-container> eingetragen.</li>
          <li><strong>C1:</strong> <code>=B1/A1</code>, bis C5 ausgefüllt → fünf Werte für <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="Q/U" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="Q"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D444">𝑄</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex="/"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2F">/</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container>.</li>
        </ul></div>
        </section>
      
        <section class="documentation-section" data-section-id="geogebra">
          <h3>2. GeoGebra-Auswertung</h3>
          <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <p>Ich bilde den Mittelwert der fünf Quotienten:</p>
        <pre><code>a=Mittel(C1:C5)</code></pre>
        <p>GeoGebra liefert <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="a\approx0{,}0415933" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="a"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D44E">𝑎</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-break size="4"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="\approx"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2248">≈</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="4" data-latex="0{,}041"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">,</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c34" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">4</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">1</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mn data-latex="5933"><mjx-c class="mjx-c35" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">5</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c39" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">9</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c33" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">3</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c33" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">3</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-math></mjx-container> in der Skalierung <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="10^{-8}\,\mathrm F" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-msup data-latex="10^{-8}"><mjx-mn data-latex="10"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-script style="vertical-align: 0.393em;"><mjx-texatom size="s" data-latex="{-8}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex="-"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2212">−</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn data-latex="8"><mjx-c class="mjx-c38">8</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-texatom></mjx-script></mjx-msup><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm F" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="F"><mjx-c class="mjx-c46">F</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container>.</p></div>
        </section>
      
        <section class="documentation-section" data-section-id="physical">
          <h3>3. Physikalische Formel und Parameter</h3>
          <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <p>Die mittlere Kapazität beträgt:</p>
        <p class="display-equation"><mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" display="true" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="C\approx0{,}0415933\cdot10^{-8}\,\mathrm F\approx416\,\mathrm{pF}." display="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="C"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D436">𝐶</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo space="4" data-latex="\approx"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2248">≈</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="4" data-latex="0{,}041"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">,</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c34" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">4</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">1</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mn data-latex="5933"><mjx-c class="mjx-c35" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">5</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c39" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">9</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c33" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">3</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c33" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">3</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mo space="3" data-latex="\cdot"><mjx-c class="mjx-c22C5">⋅</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-msup space="3" data-latex="10^{-8}"><mjx-mn data-latex="10"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-script style="vertical-align: 0.413em;"><mjx-texatom size="s" data-latex="{-8}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex="-"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2212">−</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn data-latex="8"><mjx-c class="mjx-c38">8</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-texatom></mjx-script></mjx-msup><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm F" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="F"><mjx-c class="mjx-c46">F</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom><mjx-mo space="4" data-latex="\approx"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2248">≈</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="4" data-latex="416"><mjx-c class="mjx-c34" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">4</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c36" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">6</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm{pF}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="pF"><mjx-c class="mjx-c70" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.68em;">p</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c46" style="padding-top: 0.68em;">F</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom><mjx-mo data-latex="."><mjx-c class="mjx-c2E">.</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-math></mjx-container></p>
        <p>Damit lautet das vereinfachte Modell:</p>
        <p class="display-equation"><mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" display="true" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="Q(U)\approx(416\,\mathrm{pF})\cdot U." display="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="Q"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D444">𝑄</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex="("><mjx-c class="mjx-c28">(</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex=")"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29">)</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mo space="4" data-latex="\approx"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2248">≈</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mo space="4" data-latex="("><mjx-c class="mjx-c28">(</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn data-latex="416"><mjx-c class="mjx-c34" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">4</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c36" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">6</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm{pF}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="pF"><mjx-c class="mjx-c70" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.68em;">p</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c46" style="padding-top: 0.68em;">F</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom><mjx-mo data-latex=")"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29">)</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mo space="3" data-latex="\cdot"><mjx-c class="mjx-c22C5">⋅</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi space="3" data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex="."><mjx-c class="mjx-c2E">.</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-math></mjx-container></p></div>
        </section>
      
        <section class="documentation-section" data-section-id="deviations">
          <h3>4. Abweichungen und Vergleichsregel</h3>
          <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <ul>
          <li><strong>D1:</strong> <code>=a</code>, bis D5 ausgefüllt → mittlerer Kapazitätswert.</li>
          <li><strong>E1:</strong> <code>=(C1-D1)/D1*100</code>, bis E5 ausgefüllt → Abweichungen der Einzelkapazitäten.</li>
        </ul>
        <p>Bei <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="U=50\,\mathrm V" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-break size="4"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="="><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D">=</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="4" data-latex="50"><mjx-c class="mjx-c35" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">5</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm V" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="V"><mjx-c class="mjx-c56">V</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container> liegt die Einzelkapazität um etwa <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="3{,}83\,\%" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="3"><mjx-c class="mjx-c33">3</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-texatom data-latex="{,}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-texatom><mjx-mn data-latex="83"><mjx-c class="mjx-c38" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">8</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c33" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">3</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> unter dem Mittelwert. Der größte relative Einzelfehler beträgt <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="10\,\%" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="10"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container>.</p></div>
        </section>
      
        <section class="documentation-section" data-section-id="conclusion">
          <h3>5. Begründete Schlussfolgerung</h3>
          <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <p>Die größte Streuung der Einzelkapazitäten liegt unter <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="10\,\%" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="10"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container>. Nach der Methode des größten Einzelfehlers kann sie durch Messfehler erklärt werden. Die Kapazität darf im Rahmen dieser Methode als näherungsweise konstant angesehen werden. Die Messwerte sind mit <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="Q\propto U" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="Q"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D444">𝑄</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-break size="4"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="\propto"><mjx-c class="mjx-c221D">∝</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi space="4" data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> vereinbar, beweisen die Proportionalität aber nicht.</p></div>
        </section>
      
    </div>
    <p class="documentation-paper-footnote">Muster für eine knappe Klausurdokumentation. GeoGebra-Befehle und Zellbereiche machen den Rechenweg nachvollziehbar.</p>
  </article><article id="model-proportional-linear" class="documentation-paper documentation-model-paper" data-example-id="proportional-linear" hidden="">
    <header class="documentation-paper-header">
      <div>
        <p class="mini-label">Ausgeführtes Muster</p>
        <span class="documentation-paper-kicker">Kondensator-Versuch · Lineare Regression</span>
        <h2>Direkte Proportionalität · Lineare Regression</h2>
      </div>
      <div class="documentation-paper-equation"><mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" display="true" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="Q=C\cdot U" display="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="Q"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D444">𝑄</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo space="4" data-latex="="><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D">=</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi space="4" data-latex="C"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D436">𝐶</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo space="3" data-latex="\cdot"><mjx-c class="mjx-c22C5">⋅</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi space="3" data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container></div>
    </header>
    <p class="documentation-example-introduction">Dieses Beispiel dokumentiert eine lineare Regression mit freiem y-Achsenabschnitt.</p>
    <div class="documentation-model-sections">
      
        <section class="documentation-section" data-section-id="data">
          <h3>1. Daten und Einheiten</h3>
          <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <p>Ich werte fünf Messpaare eines Kondensatorversuchs aus.</p>
        <ul>
          <li><strong>A1:A5:</strong> Spannungen <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="U" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> eingetragen; Einheit <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="\mathrm V" class="NCM-N"><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm V" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="V"><mjx-c class="mjx-c56">V</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container>.</li>
          <li><strong>B1:B5:</strong> Ladungszahlen <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="Q/(10^{-8}\,\mathrm C)" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="Q"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D444">𝑄</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex="/"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2F">/</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mo data-latex="("><mjx-c class="mjx-c28">(</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-msup data-latex="10^{-8}"><mjx-mn data-latex="10"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-script style="vertical-align: 0.393em;"><mjx-texatom size="s" data-latex="{-8}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex="-"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2212">−</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn data-latex="8"><mjx-c class="mjx-c38">8</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-texatom></mjx-script></mjx-msup><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm C" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="C"><mjx-c class="mjx-c43">C</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom><mjx-mo data-latex=")"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29">)</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-math></mjx-container> eingetragen.</li>
          <li><strong>C1:</strong> <code>=(A1,B1)</code>, bis C5 ausgefüllt → Punkte <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="(U,Q)" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mo data-latex="("><mjx-c class="mjx-c28">(</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi space="2" data-latex="Q"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D444">𝑄</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex=")"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29">)</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-math></mjx-container>.</li>
        </ul></div>
        </section>
      
        <section class="documentation-section" data-section-id="geogebra">
          <h3>2. GeoGebra-Auswertung</h3>
          <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <p>Für die Punkte C1:C5 bestimme ich eine Regressionsgerade:</p>
        <pre><code>Q=Trendlinie(C1:C5)</code></pre>
        <p>GeoGebra liefert:</p>
        <p class="display-equation"><mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" display="true" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="Q(U)=0{,}0408\,U+0{,}12." display="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="Q"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D444">𝑄</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex="("><mjx-c class="mjx-c28">(</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex=")"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29">)</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mo space="4" data-latex="="><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D">=</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="4" data-latex="0{,}040"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">,</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c34" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">4</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mn data-latex="8"><mjx-c class="mjx-c38">8</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo space="3" data-latex="+"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2B">+</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="3" data-latex="0"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-texatom data-latex="{,}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-texatom><mjx-mn data-latex="12."><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c32" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">2</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c2E" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">.</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-math></mjx-container></p>
        <p>Die numerische Gerade verwendet die Ladungsskala <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="10^{-8}\,\mathrm C" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-msup data-latex="10^{-8}"><mjx-mn data-latex="10"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-script style="vertical-align: 0.393em;"><mjx-texatom size="s" data-latex="{-8}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex="-"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2212">−</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn data-latex="8"><mjx-c class="mjx-c38">8</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-texatom></mjx-script></mjx-msup><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm C" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="C"><mjx-c class="mjx-c43">C</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container>.</p></div>
        </section>
      
        <section class="documentation-section" data-section-id="physical">
          <h3>3. Physikalische Formel und Parameter</h3>
          <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <p>Die Steigung entspricht in der verwendeten Skalierung einer Kapazität von:</p>
        <p class="display-equation"><mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" display="true" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="m=0{,}0408\cdot10^{-8}\,\mathrm F=408\,\mathrm{pF}." display="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="m"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D45A">𝑚</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo space="4" data-latex="="><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D">=</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c34" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">4</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mn data-latex="8"><mjx-c class="mjx-c38">8</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mo space="3" data-latex="\cdot"><mjx-c class="mjx-c22C5">⋅</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-msup space="3" data-latex="10^{-8}"><mjx-mn data-latex="10"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-script style="vertical-align: 0.413em;"><mjx-texatom size="s" data-latex="{-8}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex="-"><mjx-c 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm F" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="F"><mjx-c class="mjx-c46">F</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom><mjx-mo space="4" data-latex="="><mjx-c 0.677em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c38" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">8</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm{pF}"         <p>Die Gerade mit Einheiten lautet:</p>
        <p class="display-equation"><mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" display="true" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="Q(U)\approx(408\,\mathrm{pF})\cdot class="mjx-c46" style="padding-top: 0.68em;">F</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom><mjx-mo data-latex=")"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29">)</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mo space="3" data-latex="\cdot"><mjx-c data-latex="C"><mjx-c class="mjx-c43">C</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom><mjx-mo data-latex="."><mjx-c class="mjx-c2E">.</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-math></mjx-container></p></div>
        </section>
      
        <section class="documentation-section" data-section-id="deviations">
          <h3>4. Abweichungen und Vergleichsregel</h3>
          <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <ul>
          <li><strong>D1:</strong> <code>=Q(A1)</code>, bis D5 ausgefüllt → Modellwerte.</li>
          <li><strong>E1:</strong> <code>=(B1-D1)/D1*100</code>, bis E5 ausgefüllt → Modellabweichungen.</li>
        </ul>
        <p>Bei <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="U=50\,\mathrm V" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-break size="4"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="="><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D">=</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="4" data-latex="50"><mjx-c class="mjx-c35" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">5</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm V" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="V"><mjx-c class="mjx-c56">V</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container> liegt der Messwert um etwa <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="7{,}41\,\%" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="7"><mjx-c class="mjx-c37">7</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-texatom data-latex="{,}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-texatom><mjx-mn data-latex="41"><mjx-c class="mjx-c34" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">4</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">1</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> unter dem Modellwert. Der y-Achsenabschnitt entspricht am kleinsten Ladungswert <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="0{,}12/2{,}0\cdot100\,\%=6\,\%" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="0"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-texatom data-latex="{,}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-texatom><mjx-mn data-latex="12"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c32" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">2</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mo data-latex="/"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2F">/</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn data-latex="2"><mjx-c class="mjx-c32">2</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-texatom data-latex="{,}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-texatom><mjx-mn data-latex="0"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="3"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="\cdot"><mjx-c class="mjx-c22C5">⋅</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="3" data-latex="100"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-break size="4"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="="><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D">=</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="4" data-latex="6"><mjx-c class="mjx-c36">6</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container>. Beide Werte liegen unter dem größten relativen Einzelfehler von <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="10\,\%" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="10"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container>.</p></div>
        </section>
      
        <section class="documentation-section" data-section-id="conclusion">
          <h3>5. Begründete Schlussfolgerung</h3>
          <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <p>Die größte Modellabweichung und der Anteil des y-Achsenabschnitts liegen unter <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="10\,\%" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="10"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container>. Nach der Methode des größten Einzelfehlers können sie durch Messfehler erklärt werden. Der y-Achsenabschnitt darf näherungsweise vernachlässigt werden, sodass <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="Q(U)\approx(408\,\mathrm{pF})\cdot U" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="Q"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D444">𝑄</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex="("><mjx-c class="mjx-c28">(</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex=")"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29">)</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-break size="4"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="\approx"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2248">≈</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mo space="4" data-latex="("><mjx-c class="mjx-c28">(</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn data-latex="408"><mjx-c class="mjx-c34" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">4</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c38" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">8</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm{pF}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="pF"><mjx-c class="mjx-c70" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.68em;">p</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c46" style="padding-top: 0.68em;">F</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom><mjx-mo data-latex=")"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29">)</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-break size="3"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="\cdot"><mjx-c class="mjx-c22C5">⋅</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi space="3" data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> gilt. Die Messwerte sind damit mit <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="Q\propto U" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="Q"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D444">𝑄</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-break size="4"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="\propto"><mjx-c class="mjx-c221D">∝</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi space="4" data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> vereinbar. Das ist keine statistische Bestätigung eines exakt verschwindenden y-Achsenabschnitts.</p></div>
        </section>
      
    </div>
    <p class="documentation-paper-footnote">Muster für eine knappe Klausurdokumentation. GeoGebra-Befehle und Zellbereiche machen den Rechenweg nachvollziehbar.</p>

    <header class="documentation-paper-header">
      <div>
        <p class="mini-label">Ausgeführtes Muster</p>
        <span class="documentation-paper-kicker">Kondensator-Aufladung · Exponentialregression</span>
        <h2>Kondensator-Aufladung · Exponentialregression</h2>
      </div>
      <div class="documentation-paper-equation"><mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" display="true" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="\Delta U(t)=U_0e^{-t/\tau}" display="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="\Delta"><mjx-c class="mjx-c394">Δ</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex="("><mjx-c class="mjx-c28">(</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi data-latex="t"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D461">𝑡</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex=")"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29">)</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mo space="4" data-latex="="><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D">=</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-msub space="4" data-latex="U_0"><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: -0.15em; margin-left: -0.077em;"><mjx-mn size="s" data-latex="0"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-script></mjx-msub><mjx-msup data-latex="e^{-t/\tau}"><mjx-mi data-latex="e"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D452">𝑒</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: 0.413em;"><mjx-texatom size="s" data-latex="{-t/\tau}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex="-"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2212">−</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi data-latex="t"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D461">𝑡</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex="/"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2F">/</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi data-latex="\tau"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D70F">𝜏</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-script></mjx-msup></mjx-math></mjx-container></div>
    </header>
    <p class="documentation-example-introduction">Dieses Beispiel zeigt ausschließlich die Dokumentation der Auswertung von neun Messpaaren zwischen <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="0" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="0"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-math></mjx-container> und <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="80\,\mathrm s" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="80"><mjx-c class="mjx-c38" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">8</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm s" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="s"><mjx-c class="mjx-c73">s</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container>. Die Prüfung und Behandlung späterer Messwerte ist nicht Gegenstand dieses Beispiels.</p>
    <div class="documentation-model-sections">
      
        <section class="documentation-section" data-section-id="data">
          <h3>1. Daten und Einheiten</h3>
          <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <p>Ausgewertet werden neun Messpaare von <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="0" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="0"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-math></mjx-container> bis <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="80\,\mathrm s" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="80"><mjx-c class="mjx-c38" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">8</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm s" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="s"><mjx-c class="mjx-c73">s</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container>. Untersucht wird die Spannungsdifferenz <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="\Delta U=U_0-U_C" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="\Delta"><mjx-c class="mjx-c394">Δ</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-break size="4"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="="><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D">=</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-msub space="4" data-latex="U_0"><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: -0.15em; margin-left: -0.077em;"><mjx-mn size="s" data-latex="0"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-script></mjx-msub><mjx-break size="3"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="-"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2212">−</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-msub space="3" data-latex="U_C"><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: -0.15em; margin-left: -0.077em;"><mjx-mi size="s" data-latex="C"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D436">𝐶</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-script></mjx-msub></mjx-math></mjx-container> mit <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="U_0=3{,}780\,\mathrm V" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-msub data-latex="U_0"><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: -0.15em; margin-left: -0.077em;"><mjx-mn size="s" data-latex="0"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-script></mjx-msub><mjx-break size="4"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="="><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D">=</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="4" data-latex="3{,}780"><mjx-c class="mjx-c33" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.676em;">3</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.676em;">,</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c37" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.676em;">7</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c38" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.676em;">8</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.676em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm V" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="V"><mjx-c class="mjx-c56">V</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container>.</p>
        <ul>
          <li><strong>A1:A9:</strong> Zeiten <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="0,10,\ldots,80" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="0"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="2" data-latex="10"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mo space="2" data-latex="\ldots"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2026">…</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mo space="2" data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="2" data-latex="80"><mjx-c class="mjx-c38" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">8</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-math></mjx-container> aus der Messwerttabelle eingetragen; Einheit <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="\mathrm s" class="NCM-N"><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm s" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="s"><mjx-c class="mjx-c73">s</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container>.</li>
          <li><strong>B1:B9:</strong> zugehörige Kondensatorspannungen <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="U_C" class="NCM-N"><mjx-msub data-latex="U_C"><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: -0.15em; margin-left: -0.077em;"><mjx-mi size="s" data-latex="C"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D436">𝐶</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-script></mjx-msub></mjx-math></mjx-container> eingetragen; Einheit <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="\mathrm V" class="NCM-N"><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm V" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="V"><mjx-c class="mjx-c56">V</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container>.</li>
          <li><strong>C1:</strong> <code>=3.780-B1</code>, bis C9 ausgefüllt → Spannungsdifferenzen in <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="\mathrm V" class="NCM-N"><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm V" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="V"><mjx-c class="mjx-c56">V</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container>.</li>
          <li><strong>D1:</strong> <code>=(A1,C1)</code>, bis D9 ausgefüllt → Punkte aus Zeit und Spannungsdifferenz.</li>
        </ul></div>
        </section>
      
        <section class="documentation-section" data-section-id="geogebra">
          <h3>2. GeoGebra-Auswertung</h3>
          <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <p>Für die Punkte D1:D9 führe ich eine Exponentialregression durch:</p>
        <pre><code>U(x)=TrendExp(D1:D9)</code></pre>
        <p>GeoGebra liefert näherungsweise:</p>
        <p class="display-equation"><mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" display="true" style="font-size: 110.8%;"><mjx-math data-latex="U(x)=3{,}69258\,e^{-0{,}0304341x}." display="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex="("><mjx-c class="mjx-c28">(</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi data-latex="x"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D465">𝑥</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex=")"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29">)</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mo space="4" data-latex="="><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D">=</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="4" data-latex="3{,}692"><mjx-c class="mjx-c33" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">3</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">,</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c36" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">6</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c39" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">9</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c32" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">2</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mn data-latex="58"><mjx-c class="mjx-c35" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">5</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c38" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">8</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-msup data-latex="e^{-0{,}0304341x}"><mjx-mi data-latex="e"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D452">𝑒</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: 0.413em;"><mjx-texatom size="s" data-latex="{-0{,}0304341x}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex="-"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2212">−</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn data-latex="0{,}030"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">,</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c33" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">3</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mn data-latex="4341"><mjx-c class="mjx-c34" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">4</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c33" noic="true"
