        <section class="documentation-practice-section" data-section-id="deviations">
          <h3>4. Abweichungen und Vergleichsregel</h3>
          <p class="documentation-task"><strong>Dein Schreibauftrag:</strong> Dokumentiere Modellabweichung und Anteil des y-Achsenabschnitts am kleinsten Messwert.</p>
          <details>
            <summary>Was muss hinein?</summary>
            <ul><li>D und E beschrieben</li><li>7,41 % genannt</li><li>6 % mit 10 % verglichen</li></ul>
          </details>
          <details>
            <summary>Ich brauche eine Starthilfe</summary>
            <p>Die größte Modellabweichung beträgt … . Der y-Achsenabschnitt macht am kleinsten Messwert … aus.</p>
          </details>
          <details>
            <summary>Mit der Musterlösung vergleichen</summary>
            <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <ul>
          <li><strong>D1:</strong> <code>=Q(A1)</code>, bis D5 ausgefüllt → Modellwerte.</li>
          <li><strong>E1:</strong> <code>=(B1-D1)/D1*100</code>, bis E5 ausgefüllt → Modellabweichungen.</li>
        </ul>
        <p>Bei <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="U=50\,\mathrm V" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-break size="4"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="="><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D">=</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="4" data-latex="50"><mjx-c class="mjx-c35" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">5</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm V" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="V"><mjx-c class="mjx-c56">V</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container> liegt der Messwert um etwa <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="7{,}41\,\%" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="7"><mjx-c class="mjx-c37">7</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-texatom data-latex="{,}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-texatom><mjx-mn data-latex="41"><mjx-c class="mjx-c34" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">4</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">1</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> unter dem Modellwert. Der y-Achsenabschnitt entspricht am kleinsten Ladungswert <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="0{,}12/2{,}0\cdot100\,\%=6\,\%" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="0"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-texatom data-latex="{,}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-texatom><mjx-mn data-latex="12"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c32" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">2</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mo data-latex="/"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2F">/</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn data-latex="2"><mjx-c class="mjx-c32">2</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-texatom data-latex="{,}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-texatom><mjx-mn data-latex="0"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="3"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="\cdot"><mjx-c class="mjx-c22C5">⋅</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="3" data-latex="100"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-break size="4"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="="><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D">=</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="4" data-latex="6"><mjx-c class="mjx-c36">6</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container>. Beide Werte liegen unter dem größten relativen Einzelfehler von <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="10\,\%" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="10"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container>.</p></div>
          </details>
          <label class="documentation-self-check">
            <input type="checkbox" data-self-check-example="proportional-linear" data-self-check-section="deviations">
            <span>Diesen Abschnitt habe ich selbst überprüft.</span>
          </label>
        </section>
      
        <section class="documentation-practice-section" data-section-id="conclusion">
          <h3>5. Begründete Schlussfolgerung</h3>
          <p class="documentation-task"><strong>Dein Schreibauftrag:</strong> Erkläre, warum der y-Achsenabschnitt im Rahmen dieser Methode vernachlässigt wird.</p>
          <details>
            <summary>Was muss hinein?</summary>
            <ul><li>beide Vergleiche genannt</li><li>Näherung formuliert</li><li>keine exakte Bestätigung behauptet</li></ul>
          </details>
          <details>
            <summary>Ich brauche eine Starthilfe</summary>
            <p>Da … und … unter <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="10\,\%" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="10"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> liegen, darf der y-Achsenabschnitt … .</p>
          </details>
          <details>
            <summary>Mit der Musterlösung vergleichen</summary>
            <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <p>Die größte Modellabweichung und der Anteil des y-Achsenabschnitts liegen unter <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="10\,\%" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="10"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container>. Nach der Methode des größten Einzelfehlers können sie durch Messfehler erklärt werden. Der y-Achsenabschnitt darf näherungsweise vernachlässigt werden, sodass <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="Q(U)\approx(408\,\mathrm{pF})\cdot U" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="Q"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D444">𝑄</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex="("><mjx-c class="mjx-c28">(</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex=")"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29">)</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-break size="4"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="\approx"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2248">≈</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mo space="4" data-latex="("><mjx-c class="mjx-c28">(</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn data-latex="408"><mjx-c class="mjx-c34" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">4</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c38" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">8</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm{pF}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="pF"><mjx-c class="mjx-c70" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.68em;">p</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c46" style="padding-top: 0.68em;">F</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom><mjx-mo data-latex=")"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29">)</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-break size="3"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="\cdot"><mjx-c class="mjx-c22C5">⋅</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi space="3" data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> gilt. Die Messwerte sind damit mit <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="Q\propto U" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="Q"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D444">𝑄</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-break size="4"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="\propto"><mjx-c class="mjx-c221D">∝</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi space="4" data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> vereinbar. Das ist keine statistische Bestätigung eines exakt verschwindenden y-Achsenabschnitts.</p></div>
          </details>
          <label class="documentation-self-check">
            <input type="checkbox" data-self-check-example="proportional-linear" data-self-check-section="conclusion">
            <span>Diesen Abschnitt habe ich selbst überprüft.</span>
          </label>
        </section>

        <section class="documentation-practice-section" data-section-id="data">
          <h3>1. Daten und Einheiten</h3>
          <p class="documentation-task"><strong>Dein Schreibauftrag:</strong> Dokumentiere, welche neun Messpaare ausgewertet werden und wie die vier Spalten entstehen.</p>
          <details>
            <summary>Was muss hinein?</summary>
            <ul><li>Zeitraum 0 bis 80 s genannt</li><li>A und B mit Einheiten genannt</li><li>C und D mit Eingaben beschrieben</li></ul>
          </details>
          <details>
            <summary>Ich brauche eine Starthilfe</summary>
            <p>Ich werte … Messpaare von … bis … aus. In A stehen …, in B stehen … .</p>
          </details>
          <details>
            <summary>Mit der Musterlösung vergleichen</summary>
            <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <p>Ausgewertet werden neun Messpaare von <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="0" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="0"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-math></mjx-container> bis <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="80\,\mathrm s" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="80"><mjx-c class="mjx-c38" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">8</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm s" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="s"><mjx-c class="mjx-c73">s</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container>. Untersucht wird die Spannungsdifferenz <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="\Delta U=U_0-U_C" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="\Delta"><mjx-c class="mjx-c394">Δ</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-break size="4"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="="><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D">=</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-msub space="4" data-latex="U_0"><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: -0.15em; margin-left: -0.077em;"><mjx-mn size="s" data-latex="0"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-script></mjx-msub><mjx-break size="3"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="-"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2212">−</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-msub space="3" data-latex="U_C"><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: -0.15em; margin-left: -0.077em;"><mjx-mi size="s" data-latex="C"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D436">𝐶</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-script></mjx-msub></mjx-math></mjx-container> mit <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="U_0=3{,}780\,\mathrm V" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-msub data-latex="U_0"><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: -0.15em; margin-left: -0.077em;"><mjx-mn size="s" data-latex="0"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-script></mjx-msub><mjx-break size="4"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="="><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D">=</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="4" data-latex="3{,}780"><mjx-c class="mjx-c33" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.676em;">3</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.676em;">,</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c37" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.676em;">7</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c38" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.676em;">8</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.676em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm V" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="V"><mjx-c class="mjx-c56">V</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container>.</p>
        <ul>
          <li><strong>A1:A9:</strong> Zeiten <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="0,10,\ldots,80" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="0"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="2" data-latex="10"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mo space="2" data-latex="\ldots"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2026">…</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mo space="2" data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="2" data-latex="80"><mjx-c class="mjx-c38" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">8</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-math></mjx-container> aus der Messwerttabelle eingetragen; Einheit <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="\mathrm s" class="NCM-N"><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm s" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="s"><mjx-c class="mjx-c73">s</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container>.</li>
          <li><strong>B1:B9:</strong> zugehörige Kondensatorspannungen <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="U_C" class="NCM-N"><mjx-msub data-latex="U_C"><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: -0.15em; margin-left: -0.077em;"><mjx-mi size="s" data-latex="C"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D436">𝐶</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-script></mjx-msub></mjx-math></mjx-container> eingetragen; Einheit <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="\mathrm V" class="NCM-N"><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm V" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="V"><mjx-c class="mjx-c56">V</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container>.</li>
          <li><strong>C1:</strong> <code>=3.780-B1</code>, bis C9 ausgefüllt → Spannungsdifferenzen in <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="\mathrm V" class="NCM-N"><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm V" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="V"><mjx-c class="mjx-c56">V</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container>.</li>
          <li><strong>D1:</strong> <code>=(A1,C1)</code>, bis D9 ausgefüllt → Punkte aus Zeit und Spannungsdifferenz.</li>
        </ul></div>
          </details>
          <label class="documentation-self-check">
            <input type="checkbox" data-self-check-example="capacitor-exponential" data-self-check-section="data">
            <span>Diesen Abschnitt habe ich selbst überprüft.</span>
          </label>
        </section>
      
        <section class="documentation-practice-section" data-section-id="geogebra">
          <h3>2. GeoGebra-Auswertung</h3>
          <p class="documentation-task"><strong>Dein Schreibauftrag:</strong> Notiere GeoGebra-Befehl, Zellbereich und Regressionsfunktion.</p>
          <details>
            <summary>Was muss hinein?</summary>
            <ul><li>TrendExp genannt</li><li>D1:D9 genannt</li><li>Funktionswert als Spannungsdifferenz gedeutet</li></ul>
          </details>
          <details>
            <summary>Ich brauche eine Starthilfe</summary>
            <p>Für die Punkte … verwende ich … . GeoGebra liefert … .</p>
          </details>
          <details>
            <summary>Mit der Musterlösung vergleichen</summary>
            <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <p>Für die Punkte D1:D9 führe ich eine Exponentialregression durch:</p>
        <pre><code>U(x)=TrendExp(D1:D9)</code></pre>
        <p>GeoGebra liefert näherungsweise:</p>
        <p class="display-equation"><mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" display="true" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="U(x)=3{,}69258\,e^{-0{,}0304341x}." display="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex="("><mjx-c class="mjx-c28">(</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi data-latex="x"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D465">𝑥</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex=")"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29">)</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mo space="4" data-latex="="><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D">=</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="4" data-latex="3{,}692"><mjx-c class="mjx-c33" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">3</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">,</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c36" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">6</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c39" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">9</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c32" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">2</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mn data-latex="58"><mjx-c class="mjx-c35" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">5</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c38" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">8</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-msup data-latex="e^{-0{,}0304341x}"><mjx-mi data-latex="e"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D452">𝑒</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: 0.413em;"><mjx-texatom size="s" data-latex="{-0{,}0304341x}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex="-"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2212">−</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn data-latex="0{,}030"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">,</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c33" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">3</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mn data-latex="4341"><mjx-c class="mjx-c34" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.677em;">4</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c33" noic="true" data-latex="x"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D465">𝑥</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-script></mjx-msup><mjx-mo data-latex="."><mjx-c class="mjx-c2E">.</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-math></mjx-container></p>
        <p><mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="x" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="x"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D465">𝑥</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> steht für den Zahlenwert der Zeit in Sekunden. Die Funktion <code>U</code> berechnet den Zahlenwert der Spannungsdifferenz in Volt, nicht die Kondensatorspannung.</p></div>
          </details>
          <label class="documentation-self-check">
            <input type="checkbox" data-self-check-example="capacitor-exponential" data-self-check-section="geogebra">
            <span>Diesen Abschnitt habe ich selbst überprüft.</span>
          </label>
        </section>
      
        <section class="documentation-practice-section" data-section-id="physical">
          <h3>3. Physikalische Formel und Parameter</h3>
          <p class="documentation-task"><strong>Dein Schreibauftrag:</strong> Bestimme zuerst <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="\tau" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="\tau"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D70F">𝜏</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> aus dem Exponenten. Schreibe dann die Formel mit <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="t/\tau" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="t"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D461">𝑡</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex="/"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2F">/</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi data-latex="\tau"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D70F">𝜏</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> und Einheiten auf.</p>
          <details>
            <summary>Was muss hinein?</summary>
            <ul><li>negatives Vorzeichen berücksichtigt</li><li><mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="\tau" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="\tau"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D70F">𝜏</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> in Sekunden berechnet</li><li><mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="\Delta U(t)" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="\Delta"><mjx-c class="mjx-c394">Δ</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex="("><mjx-c class="mjx-c28">(</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi data-latex="t"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D461">𝑡</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex=")"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29">)</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-math></mjx-container>, nicht <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="U_C(t)" class="NCM-N"><mjx-msub data-latex="U_C"><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: -0.15em; margin-left: -0.077em;"><mjx-mi size="s" data-latex="C"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D436">𝐶</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-script></mjx-msub><mjx-mo data-latex="("><mjx-c class="mjx-c28">(</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi data-latex="t"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D461">𝑡</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex=")"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29">)</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-math></mjx-container>, modelliert</li></ul>
          </details>
          <details>
            <summary>Ich brauche eine Starthilfe</summary>
            <p>Aus <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="k=-1/\tau" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="k"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D458">𝑘</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-break size="4"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="="><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D">=</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-break size="4"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="-"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2212">−</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn data-latex="1"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31">1</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mo data-latex="/"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2F">/</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi data-latex="\tau"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D70F">𝜏</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> folgt … . Damit lautet die physikalische Modellfunktion … .</p>
          </details>
          <details>
            <summary>Mit der Musterlösung vergleichen</summary>
            <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <p>Die Theorie enthält den Exponenten <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="-t/\tau" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mo data-latex="-"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2212">−</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi data-latex="t"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D461">𝑡</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex="/"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2F">/</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi data-latex="\tau"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D70F">𝜏</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container>.         <p>Der Faktor trägt die Einheit Volt. <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="t" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="t"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D461">𝑡</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> und <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="\tau" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="\tau"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D70F">𝜏</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> werden in derselben Zeiteinheit eingesetzt; deshalb ist <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="t/\tau" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="t"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D461">𝑡</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex="/"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2F">/</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi data-latex="\tau"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D70F">𝜏</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> einheitenfrei.</p>

Außerdem ist <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="t_{1/2}=\tau\ln2\approx22{,}78\,\mathrm s" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-msub data-latex="t_{1/2}"><mjx-mi data-latex="t"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D461">𝑡</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: -0.177em;"><mjx-texatom size="s" data-latex="{1/2}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mn data-latex="1"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31">1</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-mo data-latex="/"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2F">/</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn data-latex="2"><mjx-c class="mjx-c32">2</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-texatom></mjx-script></mjx-msub><mjx-break size="4"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="="><mjx-c class="mjx-c3D">=</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi space="4" data-latex="\tau"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D70F">𝜏</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi space="2" data-latex="\ln"><mjx-c class="mjx-c6C" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.694em;">l</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c6E" style="padding-top: 0.694em;">n</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo><mjx-c class="mjx-c2061">⁡</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="2" data-latex="2"><mjx-c class="mjx-c32">2</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="4"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="\approx"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2248">≈</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="4" data-latex="22"><mjx-c class="mjx-c32" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">2</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c32" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">2</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-texatom data-latex="{,}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-texatom><mjx-mn data-latex="78"><mjx-c class="mjx-c37" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.676em;">7</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c38" style="padding-top: 0.676em;">8</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm s" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="s"><mjx-c class="mjx-c73">s</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container>. Der Regressionsanfangswert wird dabei nicht stillschweigend durch <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="U_0" class="NCM-N"><mjx-msub data-latex="U_0"><mjx-mi data-latex="U"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D448">𝑈</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: -0.15em; margin-left: -0.077em;"><mjx-mn size="s" data-latex="0"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-script></mjx-msub></mjx-math></mjx-container> ersetzt.</p></details></div>
          </details>
          <label class="documentation-self-check">
            <input type="checkbox" data-self-check-example="capacitor-exponential" data-self-check-section="physical">
            <span>Diesen Abschnitt habe ich selbst überprüft.</span>
          </label>
        </section>
      
        <section class="documentation-practice-section" data-section-id="deviations">
          <h3>4. Abweichungen und Vergleichsregel</h3>
          <p class="documentation-task"><strong>Dein Schreibauftrag:</strong> Dokumentiere die zwei Vergleichsspalten, ein eingesetztes Beispiel und den größten relativen Einzelfehler.</p>
          <details>
            <summary>Was muss hinein?</summary>
            <ul><li>E und F beschrieben</li><li>2,61 % mit Richtung genannt</li><li>2,31 % und 10 % genannt</li></ul>
          </details>
          <details>
            <summary>Ich brauche eine Starthilfe</summary>
            <p>Die größte Modellabweichung tritt bei … auf. Dort liegt der Messwert … dem Modellwert.</p>
          </details>
          <details>
            <summary>Mit der Musterlösung vergleichen</summary>
            <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <ul>
          <li><strong>E1:</strong> <code>=U(A1)</code>, bis E9 ausgefüllt → Modellwerte.</li>
          <li><strong>F1:</strong> <code>=(C1-E1)/E1*100</code>, bis F9 ausgefüllt → relative Modellabweichungen.</li>
        </ul>
        <p>Die größte Modellabweichung tritt bei <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="80\,\mathrm s" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="80"><mjx-c class="mjx-c38" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">8</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm s" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="s"><mjx-c class="mjx-c73">s</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container> auf:</p>
        <p class="display-equation"><mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" display="true" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math         <p>Die gemessene Spannungsdifferenz liegt dort um etwa <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="2{,}61\,\%" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="2"><mjx-c class="mjx-c32">2</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-texatom data-latex="{,}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-texatom><mjx-mn data-latex="61"><mjx-c class="mjx-c36" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">6</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> über dem Modellwert.</p>
        <p>Für die ausgewerteten Daten gilt <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math           </label>
        </section>
      
        <section class="documentation-practice-section" data-section-id="conclusion">
          <h3>5. Begründete Schlussfolgerung</h3>
          <p class="documentation-task"><strong>Dein Schreibauftrag:</strong> Formuliere ein begründetes, aber vorsichtiges Urteil für den Zeitraum von 0 bis 80 s.</p>
          <details>
            <summary>Was muss hinein?</summary>
            <ul><li>beide Abweichungen mit 10 % verglichen</li><li><mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="\tau" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="\tau"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D70F">𝜏</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> genannt</li><li>kein Beweis behauptet</li></ul>
          </details>
          <details>
            <summary>Ich brauche eine Starthilfe</summary>
            <p>Da … und … unter <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="10\,\%" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="10"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> liegen, sind die Messwerte von … bis … mit … vereinbar.</p>
          </details>
          <details>
            <summary>Mit der Musterlösung vergleichen</summary>
            <div class="documentation-model-copy">
        <p>Die Anfangswertabweichung von etwa <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="2{,}31\,\%" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="2"><mjx-c class="mjx-c32">2</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-texatom data-latex="{,}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-texatom><mjx-mn data-latex="31"><mjx-c class="mjx-c33" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">3</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> und die größte Modellabweichung von etwa <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="2{,}61\,\%" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="2"><mjx-c class="mjx-c32">2</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-texatom data-latex="{,}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-texatom><mjx-mn data-latex="61"><mjx-c class="mjx-c36" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">6</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> liegen unter <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="10\,\%" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="10"><mjx-c class="mjx-c31" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">1</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-mi data-latex="\%"><mjx-c class="mjx-c25">%</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container>. Nach der Methode des größten Einzelfehlers können sie durch Messfehler erklärt werden. Die Messwerte von <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="0" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="0"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-math></mjx-container> bis <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="80\,\mathrm s" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="80"><mjx-c class="mjx-c38" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">8</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm s" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="s"><mjx-c class="mjx-c73">s</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container> sind mit einem exponentiellen Aufladevorgang und <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="\tau\approx32{,}86\,\mathrm s" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="\tau"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D70F">𝜏</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-break size="4"> </mjx-break><mjx-mo data-latex="\approx"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2248">≈</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn space="4" data-latex="32"><mjx-c class="mjx-c33" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">3</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c32" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">2</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-texatom data-latex="{,}" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo data-latex=","><mjx-c class="mjx-c2C">,</mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-texatom><mjx-mn data-latex="86"><mjx-c class="mjx-c38" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">8</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c36" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">6</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm s" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="s"><mjx-c class="mjx-c73">s</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container> vereinbar. Ein Beweis des Modells oder die Bestätigung eines bestimmten Sollwerts für <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="\tau" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="\tau"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D70F">𝜏</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> folgt daraus nicht.</p></div>
          </details>
          <label class="documentation-self-check">
            <input type="checkbox" data-self-check-example="capacitor-exponential" data-self-check-section="conclusion">
            <span>Diesen Abschnitt habe ich selbst überprüft.</span>
          </label>
        </section>
      
    </div>
  </article></section>

      </div>

    </header>
    <p>Dieses Beispiel dokumentiert sechs Messpaare aus dem Coulomb-Versuch.</p>
    
      <section class="documentation-worksheet-section ">
        <h3>1. Daten und Einheiten</h3>
        <p><strong>Schreibauftrag:</strong> Halte die beiden Messgrößen, ihre Einheiten und die Punktspalte fest.</p>
        <div class="worksheet-lines" aria-hidden="true"></div>
      </section>
    
      <section class="documentation-worksheet-section ">

        <div class="worksheet-lines" aria-hidden="true"></div>
      </section>
    
      <section class="documentation-worksheet-section ">
        <h3>3. Physikalische Formel und Parameter</h3>
        <p><strong>Schreibauftrag:</strong> Übertrage die numerische GeoGebra-Funktion in eine Formel mit den physikalischen Größen und Einheiten.</p>
        <div class="worksheet-lines" aria-hidden="true"></div>
      </section>
    
      <section class="documentation-worksheet-section worksheet-page-break">
        <h3>4. Abweichungen und Vergleichsregel</h3>
        <p><strong>Schreibauftrag:</strong> Dokumentiere die zwei Formeln für Modellwerte und Abweichungen sowie einen aussagekräftigen Vergleich.</p>
        <div class="worksheet-lines" aria-hidden="true"></div>
      </section>
    
      <section class="documentation-worksheet-section ">
        <h3>5. Begründete Schlussfolgerung</h3>
        <p><strong>Schreibauftrag:</strong> Formuliere eine vorsichtige Aussage zum Modell und nenne zugleich seine Grenze.</p>
        <div class="worksheet-lines" aria-hidden="true"></div>

        <div class="worksheet-lines" aria-hidden="true"></div>
      </section>
    
  </article><article id="worksheet-capacitor-exponential" class="documentation-worksheet-panel" data-example-id="capacitor-exponential">
    <header class="documentation-paper-header">
      <div>
        <p class="mini-label">Übungsblatt · auf Papier ausfüllen</p>
        <span class="documentation-paper-kicker">Kondensator-Aufladung · Exponentialregression</span>
        <h1>So dokumentiere ich die Auswertung</h1>
        <h2>Kondensator-Aufladung · Exponentialregression</h2>
      </div>
      <div class="worksheet-meta"><span>Name: ____________________</span><span>Datum: ____________________</span></div>
    </header>
    <p>Dieses Beispiel zeigt ausschließlich die Dokumentation der Auswertung von neun Messpaaren zwischen <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="0" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="0"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-math></mjx-container> und <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="80\,\mathrm s" breakable="true" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mn data-latex="80"><mjx-c class="mjx-c38" noic="true" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">8</mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c30" style="padding-top: 0.666em;">0</mjx-c></mjx-mn><mjx-break size="0"> </mjx-break><mjx-mspace data-latex="\," style="width: 0.167em;"></mjx-mspace><mjx-texatom data-latex="\mathrm s" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi data-latex="s"><mjx-c class="mjx-c73">s</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math></mjx-container>. Die Prüfung und Behandlung späterer Messwerte ist nicht Gegenstand dieses Beispiels.</p>
    
      <section class="documentation-worksheet-section ">
        <h3>1. Daten und Einheiten</h3>
        <p><strong>Schreibauftrag:</strong> Dokumentiere, welche neun Messpaare ausgewertet werden und wie die vier Spalten entstehen.</p>
        <div class="worksheet-lines" aria-hidden="true"></div>
      </section>
    
      <section class="documentation-worksheet-section ">
        <h3>2. GeoGebra-Auswertung</h3>
        <p><strong>Schreibauftrag:</strong> Notiere GeoGebra-Befehl, Zellbereich und Regressionsfunktion.</p>
        <div class="worksheet-lines" aria-hidden="true"></div>
      </section>
    
      <section class="documentation-worksheet-section ">
        <h3>3. Physikalische Formel und Parameter</h3>
        <p><strong>Schreibauftrag:</strong> Bestimme zuerst <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="\tau" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="\tau"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D70F">𝜏</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> aus dem Exponenten. Schreibe dann die Formel mit <mjx-container class="MathJax" jax="CHTML" overflow="linebreak" style="font-size: 113.1%;"><mjx-math data-latex="t/\tau" class="NCM-N"><mjx-mi data-latex="t"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D461">𝑡</mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo data-latex="/"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2F">/</mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi data-latex="\tau"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D70F">𝜏</mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math></mjx-container> und Einheiten auf.</p>
        <div class="worksheet-lines" aria-hidden="true"></div>
      </section>
    
      <section class="documentation-worksheet-section worksheet-page-break">
        <h3>4. Abweichungen und Vergleichsregel</h3>
        <p><strong>Schreibauftrag:</strong> Dokumentiere die zwei Vergleichsspalten, ein eingesetztes Beispiel und den größten relativen Einzelfehler.</p>
        <div class="worksheet-lines" aria-hidden="true"></div>
      </section>
    
      <section class="documentation-worksheet-section ">
        <h3>5. Begründete Schlussfolgerung</h3>
        <p><strong>Schreibauftrag:</strong> Formuliere ein begründetes, aber vorsichtiges Urteil für den Zeitraum von 0 bis 80 s.</p>
        <div class="worksheet-lines" aria-hidden="true"></div>
      </section>
